  {
    "role": "system",
    "content": "/no_think\nYou are a board-certified psychiatrist interviewing a patient to reach a diagnosis.\nYour goal is to reach the correct diagnosis within 20 turns of talking with the patient. From the dialogue so far and your candidates from the previous turn, list the disorders this patient could have.\n\n[Current Turn]\n11\n\n[Previous Candidates]\nF33.1, F33.9\n\n[Dialogue History]\nDoctor: Hello, welcome to the clinic. Can you tell me what's been troubling you and what brings you in today?\n\nPatient: I guess… I got in over my head. I took on this historical society fundraiser and started acting like I could run the whole thing myself—quilt designs, calling volunteers, making schedules. I promised deadlines I really couldn’t meet.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down or losing interest in things you usually enjoy?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I guess I have. I used to like going out to check on my garden, and working on my own quilt pieces. Lately I just sort of look at the fabric, or the seed catalogs, and… nothing really catches me.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down or losing interest in things most of the day, nearly every day, or does it come and go more randomly?\n\nPatient: Most days, I think. It’s not just once in a while. I’ll look at my quilt fabric or the seed catalogs, things I used to enjoy, and… there’s just not much there.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling more tired than usual, or like you don’t have any energy to do things?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… maybe a little. I haven’t really kept track. Some days I just don’t get much done.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you’re not good enough, or blaming yourself for things more than usual?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I suppose so. I keep turning things over in my head—late orders, things I snapped about with the volunteers. It feels like I let the church and the historical society down… maybe the whole town, really.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt down or lost interest in most things for most of the day, nearly every day, for at least two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: Yes… it’s been more than two weeks. Since the orders got behind and things blew up with the volunteers, I’ve felt kind of stuck like this. It’s been… maybe nine months now.\n\nDoctor: Between this time you’re describing and any previous periods when you felt down, have you had at least two consecutive months where you felt more like your usual self without any significant symptoms of depression?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I haven’t kept track of it that way… I guess there were better stretches, but I don’t know if it was two whole months or anything.\n\nDoctor: Looking back over the past nine months, was there any time when you felt like your usual self for at least two full months without being significantly affected by these symptoms?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think so. Since the orders fell behind and things got bad with the volunteers, I’ve kind of stayed in that low, guilty place. It’s been about nine months.\n\nDoctor: Before this nine-month period, can you recall if there was ever a time when you felt like your usual self for at least two full months without being affected by symptoms like low mood or guilt?\n\nPatient: I… I don’t really know. Before this, things are kind of blurry when I try to think back. I can’t say for sure if there was a full two months like that.\n\nDoctor: Before this current period of feeling down, have you ever had a time when you felt significantly low or lost interest in things for at least two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I mean… I’ve had times that were hard, but I don’t really remember if it was like that for two weeks straight before this.\n\nDoctor: Before this most recent time of feeling down, can you recall if there was ever a period lasting at least two weeks where you felt significantly low or lost interest in things, even if it wasn’t as long as this current time?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about before this time. Lately, though… I used to really like checking my garden and working on my quilt entries. Now I just look at the fabric or the seed catalogs and… there’s not much there.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"

From the dialogue so far and your candidates from the previous turn, list the disorders this patient could have.\n\n[Current Turn]\n15\n\n[Previous Candidates]\nF33.1, F33.9\n\n[Dialogue History]\nDoctor: Hello, welcome to the clinic. Can you tell me what's been troubling you and what brings you in today?\n\nPatient: I guess… I got in over my head. I took on this historical society fundraiser and started acting like I could run the whole thing myself—quilt designs, calling volunteers, making schedules. I promised deadlines I really couldn’t meet.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down or losing interest in things you usually enjoy?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I guess I have. I used to like going out to check on my garden, and working on my own quilt pieces. Lately I just sort of look at the fabric, or the seed catalogs, and… nothing really catches me.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down or losing interest in things most of the day, nearly every day, or does it come and go more randomly?\n\nPatient: Most days, I think. It’s not just once in a while. I’ll look at my quilt fabric or the seed catalogs, things I used to enjoy, and… there’s just not much there.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling more tired than usual, or like you don’t have any energy to do things?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… maybe a little. I haven’t really kept track. Some days I just don’t get much done.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling like you’re not good enough, or blaming yourself for things more than usual?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I suppose so. I keep turning things over in my head—late orders, things I snapped about with the volunteers. It feels like I let the church and the historical society down… maybe the whole town, really.\n\nDoctor: Have you felt down or lost interest in most things for most of the day, nearly every day, for at least two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: Yes… it’s been more than two weeks. Since the orders got behind and things blew up with the volunteers, I’ve felt kind of stuck like this. It’s been… maybe nine months now.\n\nDoctor: Between this time you’re describing and any previous periods when you felt down, have you had at least two consecutive months where you felt more like your usual self without any significant symptoms of depression?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I haven’t kept track of it that way… I guess there were better stretches, but I don’t know if it was two whole months or anything.\n\nDoctor: Looking back over the past nine months, was there any time when you felt like your usual self for at least two full months without being significantly affected by these symptoms?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think so. Since the orders fell behind and things got bad with the volunteers, I’ve kind of stayed in that low, guilty place. It’s been about nine months.\n\nDoctor: Before this nine-month period, can you recall if there was ever a time when you felt like your usual self for at least two full months without being affected by symptoms like low mood or guilt?\n\nPatient: I… I don’t really know. Before this, things are kind of blurry when I try to think back. I can’t say for sure if there was a full two months like that.\n\nDoctor: Before this current period of feeling down, have you ever had a time when you felt significantly low or lost interest in things for at least two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I mean… I’ve had times that were hard, but I don’t really remember if it was like that for two weeks straight before this.\n\nDoctor: Before this most recent time of feeling down, can you recall if there was ever a period lasting at least two weeks where you felt significantly low or lost interest in things, even if it wasn’t as long as this current time?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about before this time. Lately, though… I used to really like checking my garden and working on my quilt entries. Now I just look at the fabric or the seed catalogs and… there’s not much there.\n\nDoctor: Before this current nine-month period, was there ever a time when you felt down or lost interest in things for at least two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: I can’t really say. Before these nine months, it’s hard for me to pin down whether anything lasted two weeks like that.\n\nDoctor: Before these nine months, was there ever a time when you felt down or lost interest in things for at least two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: I really don’t know. I’m trying to remember, but nothing clear comes to mind from before these last months.\n\nDoctor: Before these last nine months, have you ever had a period where you felt down or lost interest in things for at least two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: I’m sorry, I just… I can’t really tell. Before this stretch, I don’t have a clear memory of anything like that lasting that long.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period in your life, before these last nine months, when you felt down or lost interest in things most of the day, nearly every day, for at least two weeks in a row?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know. I’ve tried to think back, but before these last nine months, I just can’t say clearly whether it was like that for two whole weeks.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. Lately I just sort of look at the fabric, or the seed catalogs, and… nothing really catches me.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down or losing interest in things most of the day, nearly every day, or does it come and go more randomly?\n\nPatient: Most days, I think.
